Enter the Zamorak area of the God Wars Dungeon	interface	generalUI	skillGuide	
Mining allows you to get various minerals from the world for use in other skills, primarily Smithing.	interface	generalUI	skillGuide	
To mine a rock, simply interact with it while holding a pickaxe you have the level to use and you'll start mining it. Eventually, you'll collect the ore and the rock will deplete. Low-level rocks will replenish themselves fairly quickly, but higher-level ones will take longer and longer to come back.	interface	generalUI	skillGuide

Regular damage from most sources.	interface	generalUI	skillGuide	
Regular damage variant shown when you deal your maximum damage. Most damage hitsplats have a highlighted max hit version.	interface	generalUI	skillGuide	
Miss/block - No damage dealt.	interface	generalUI	skillGuide	
Members: Boat damage - Shows damage to a boat.	interface	generalUI	skillGuide	
Members: Shield damage - Shows damage to a boss' shield or that a player was protected from an attack. Example: The Hueycoatl's tail shield.	interface	generalUI	skillGuide	
Members: Armour damage - Shows damage to a boss' armour. Example: Zalcano's armour.	interface	generalUI	skillGuide	
Members: Poise damage - Shows damage to a boss' poise, usually used to transition a boss to another phase. Example: The Wardens in the Tombs of Amascut.	interface	generalUI	skillGuide	
Members: Corrosion damage - Deals damage reduced by armour. May also apply a corrosion status effect, dealing damage in regular intervals. Example: Shellbane gryphon.	interface	generalUI	skillGuide	
Members: Poison - Hitpoint damage dealt in regular intervals, decreasing in potency over time. Can be cured with an anti-poison effect.	interface	generalUI	skillGuide	
Members: Venom - A more powerful form of Poison. Can be cured with mutliple anti-poison effects or a single anti-venom effect.	interface	generalUI	skillGuide	
Members: Bleed damage - Hitpoint damage dealt in regular intervals. Some types of bleed are more potent while moving. Can be cured with an anti-bleed effect. Example: Custodian Stalkers.	interface	generalUI	skillGuide	
Members: Burn damage - Hitpoint damage dealt in regular intervals. Stacks up to . Example: Burning Claws special attack.	interface	generalUI	skillGuide	
Members: Burn damage - Hitpoint damage dealt in regular intervals. Stacks up to <5>. Example: Burning Claws special attack.	interface	generalUI	skillGuide	
Members: Disease stat drain - Drains a stat in regular intervals. Can be reduced/ cured with a cure disease effect. Example: Zogres in Jiggig.	interface	generalUI	skillGuide	
Members: Disease stat drain - Drains a stat in regular intervals. Can be reduced/cured with a cure disease effect. Example: Zogres in Jiggig.	interface	generalUI	skillGuide	
Members: Disease damage - If the stat being drained by an active Disease reaches 1, you start taking Hitpoint damage instead. Can be reduced/cured with a cure disease effect.	interface	generalUI	skillGuide	
Members: Disease damage - If the stat being drained by an active Disease reaches <Num0>, you start taking Hitpoint damage instead. Can be reduced/cured with a cure disease effect.	interface	generalUI	skillGuide	
Members: Doom - Shows amount of Doom stacks. A certain amount instantly kills the player. Example: The Fortis Colosseum Doom modifier.	interface	generalUI	skillGuide	
Members: Healing from certain sources. Example: The Blood Moon in Neypotzli.	interface	generalUI	skillGuide	
Members: Prayer drain - Shows drain to a player's Prayer. Example: Frost Nagua in the Ruins of Tapoyauik.	interface	generalUI	skillGuide	
Members: Corruption - Drains Prayer points over time. Examples: Corruption spells and the Phantom Muspah encounter.	interface	generalUI	skillGuide	
Members: Cold - Drains warmth. Example: The cold in the Wintertodt encounter.	interface	generalUI	skillGuide	
Members: Charge - Shows something is being charged. Example: Totems in The Nightmare of Ashihama encounter.	interface	generalUI	skillGuide	
Members: Uncharge - Shows charge is being removed from something. Example: Totems in the Nightmare of Ashihama encounter.	interface	generalUI	skillGuide	
Members: Alternate charge - Shows something is being charged. Example: Kephri's shield restore in the Tombs of Amascut.	interface	generalUI	skillGuide	
Members: Alternate uncharge - Shows charge is being removed from something. Example: Crocodiles draining the Palm of Resourcefulness in the Tombs of Amascut.	interface	generalUI	skillGuide

Making bread:	interface	generalUI	skillGuide	
Pick some grain and take it to a windmill to make flour.	interface	generalUI	skillGuide	
Use a pot to collect the flour you have made.	interface	generalUI	skillGuide	
Fill a bucket or jug with water from a sink or fountain.	interface	generalUI	skillGuide	
Mix the flour and water to make some bread dough.	interface	generalUI	skillGuide	
Cook the dough by using it on a range.	interface	generalUI	skillGuide	
Making a pie:	interface	generalUI	skillGuide	
Mix flour and water to make pastry dough.	interface	generalUI	skillGuide	
Place the dough in an empty pie dish.	interface	generalUI	skillGuide	
Use your choice of filling on the empty pie.	interface	generalUI	skillGuide	
Cook the pie by using it on a range.	interface	generalUI	skillGuide	
Members: A Pie Recipe Book lists various recipes you may like to try.	interface	generalUI	skillGuide	
Making a pizza:	interface	generalUI	skillGuide	
Mix flour and water to make a pizza base.	interface	generalUI	skillGuide	
Add a tomato to the pizza.	interface	generalUI	skillGuide

Level <Num0> Farming allows you to make a scarecrow and place it in a flower patch. Scarecrows ward sweetcorn from being attacked by birds, which helps to prevent disease.  How to make a scarecrow: <Num1>. Fill an empty sack with straw from a bale. <Num2>. Drive the hay sack onto a bronze spear. <Num3>. Place a watermelon on top as a head. <Num4>. Stand the scarecrow in an empty flower patch.	interface	generalUI	skillGuide

You must complete the Pandemonium quest to unlock the Sailing skill.	interface	generalUI	skillGuide	
Sailing gives you the ability to captain a boat and sail the seas of Gielinor. Out at sea, you'll find all kinds of things to do. You can salvage shipwrecks for sunken treasure, test yourself in Barracuda Trials, or explore uncharted islands, to name but a few possibilities.	interface	generalUI	skillGuide	
As your Sailing level increases, you'll be able to own more boats, build better facilities on your boats, hire more crewmates, dock at more locations, and much more!	interface	generalUI	skillGuide
